  - *Why.* Every 40/10 lever read needed two seeds (the thin-pair spread is ~0.7, §242 / §247), and v10's probe keeps are 0.8 and 0.6. On the reward's own view at 40/10 (the logged "fixed target: episode ends … return", r56-w4), sens − mild is **+2.56** (κ 0.6, seed 42: −3.30 / −5.86), **+2.10** (κ 0.6, seed 43: −2.78 / −4.88; two-seed **+2.33**) and **+2.14** (κ 0.8, seed 42: −0.80 / −2.94).
  - *Cells.* Wave 16's recipe exactly (walk FT 12/4, thin, 6 passes, P, 100-ep final FT + origin): sens and mild at κ 0.6 with `SPECTRA_SEED=43` (**sens-k060-ft12-s43**, **mild-k060-ft12-s43**), and sens and mild at κ 0.8 (`param:0.8`), seed 42 (**sens-k080-ft12**, **mild-k080-ft12**). Sens in `tree_v10h` with the 5-rate menu (§227 / §230's), mild in `tree_v10` (sbatch only).
  - *Call.* d = sens − mild return at r56-w4, both at 12/4. At κ 0.6 wave 16's call is read on the **two-seed mean**: VISIBLE ≥ +1.0, HIDDEN ≤ +0.3, PARTIAL between. Where the seed-42 read and the two-seed read disagree, the two-seed read stands. κ 0.8 gets the same bars on seed 42 and is reported beside. If it disagrees with κ 0.6, the write-up says at which keep the budget hides the lever.
  - *Reported.* The lever's survival at 12/4 as d(12/4) ÷ d(40/10) at each κ, TEST walk and final for every arm, and r20-w2 as the guard.
  - *Start check.* As wave 16, plus `SPECTRA_SEED': '43'` on the seed-43 pair and `param:0.8` on the κ 0.8 pair. Not a train and not a v10 TEST.
- **Wave 16 (registered 12:10, before the 12:15 submit): the allocation lever under v10's training budget.**
  - *Why.* M1-v10 is FLAT (ops §248). The frozen actor plays 0.9 or skip and lands on mild's architecture, while a non-learned sens allocation is +2.54 over mild at κ 0.6 on two seeds (§247). v10's return telescopes to the val Δacc at the target (`fortify.py`), so its reward favours better recovery at equal size. But v10 trains with walk FT **12/4**, and every lever read so far is at 40/10. If the lever is small at 12/4, v10's reward barely saw it.
  - *Cells.* Sens α 0.5 (`tree_v10h`, §230's recipe, 5-rate menu) and mild-landed (`tree_v10`, §212's recipe), thin pair, landed κ 0.6, seed 42. The only change is `SPECTRA_NUM_EPOCHS=12 SPECTRA_FINETUNE_PATIENCE=4`; P, loader crop+flip, 6 passes, 100-ep final FT + origin and deterministic eval stay. Loader crop+flip as in every TEST walk; v10's train used the GPU variant.
  - *Call (r56-w4; the reward's view = in-walk val Δacc at the landed point).* d = sens − mild. **VISIBLE** if d ≥ +1.0: a WIN-sized lever was in v10's reward, so FLAT is a learning failure (exploration, credit assignment or representation), not the recipe. **HIDDEN** if d ≤ +0.3: the training budget hides the lever, so FLAT was predictable from the recipe, and a next train needs a longer walk FT or a different reward. **PARTIAL** in between. One seed: on this net seed spreads reach 0.7 pp (§247), so within 0.3 of a bar = unresolved. Reported: TEST walk and final-FT TEST for both arms, the same val d at 40/10 (§230 vs §212), and r20-w2.
  - *Start check.* The env line shows `SPECTRA_NUM_EPOCHS': '12'` and `SPECTRA_FINETUNE_PATIENCE': '4'`; walk FT lines read `Epoch …/12`; the sens job prints its `[alloc]` plan line.
  - Not a train and not a v10 TEST; it does not change ops' M1-v10 call.

### Lead 1 (zero GPU): group-level NAP-F vs A0 sensitivity. Call: does not correlate; C not run

Spearman ρ across a net's groups between A0's sensitivity and each group-level statistic. Groups are joined on `cut_plan` keys; p-values come from 5,000 permutations.

| Net (S0/S2 cell ↔ A0 job) | Groups | NAP-F group mean | Ablation, summed over the half a 0.5 cut removes | Taylor, same half | Depth | Width |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DepGraph R56 C10 (21945105 ↔ 22127528; in S1's fit) | 30 | +0.25 (p 0.19) | **+0.77** (p 0.0002) | **+0.77** | +0.12 | +0.27 |
| chenyaofo VGG-16 C10 (21945106 ↔ 22127529; in S1's fit) | 15 | −0.47 (p 0.08) | **+0.86** (p 0.0004) | −0.06 | −0.78 | −0.84 |
| chenyaofo R56 C100 (21982335 ↔ 22155644; held out from S1) | 30 | +0.38 (p 0.04) | **+0.92** (p 0.0002) | **+0.89** | +0.48 | +0.61 |

- **Call.** NAP-F's group mean changes sign between nets and follows depth and width, its only group-level inputs. By construction it cannot rank groups: S1's label is the within-group rank of the ablation oracle, and its features are normalised within each group (group-mean SD ≈ 0.01). The descriptor stays per-channel; the v10 state only. C, a NAP-F-weighted allocation, is not sbatched.
- **Insight.** The group-level information sits in the oracle itself. Single-channel ablation, summed over the channels a half cut removes, tracks A0's group cut on all three nets (ρ 0.77 / 0.86 / 0.92), so group damage is close to additive over channels. First-order Taylor tracks it on both ResNets but not on VGG-16. A0's measured sensitivity, already v10's state channel, stays the allocation descriptor. Taylor-half is a ResNet-only cheap stand-in, if the measurement ever costs too much.

### Lead 2 (zero GPU): Budget STOP census on 21940311 (300 train episodes, ep 0–299)

- **Budget cuts.** 5,909 in total (`budget action:` lines = `prune` events).

  | Share removed | All cuts | Last 60 episodes |
  |---|---|---|
  | 0.04 | 79.5 % | 97.5 % (1,099) |
  | 0.01 | 17.5 % | 2.5 % (28) |
  | 0.02 | 3.0 % | 0 |

- **STOP.** 74 explicit STOPs: `step` events with `stop=1` in `agent_train` mode, from the run's event stream.
  - By 50-episode bucket: 31 / 15 / 16 / 9 / 3 / 0. The last is at episode 231.
  - Keep at STOP: median 0.951 (range 0.287–1.000). 24 came at keep 1.000, six of them at step 0, and 53 of 74 were above keep 0.80.
  - After episode 199: three STOPs, at 0.655 / 0.857 / 0.776.
- **Against ops' expectation.**
  - "STOP never an agent action" is false (74 STOPs).
  - "Remove 0.0400 dominates" is true.
  - "No net parked above keep 0.80 as a STOP" is false overall (53), but holds for the last ~70 episodes.
- **Reading.** STOP was explored and then extinguished; it never became a size choice. The policy converged to "remove 4 % until the walk ends": the same one-action collapse §200 found in every TESTed actor. This is consistent with the NO-GO on the Budget resume and on TESTing ep0251. No action.

### D-PROXY: do cheap proxies rank sampled plans the way the final fine-tune does? (`docs/LEARNING_PROGRAM_OCT8.md` §4; `tree_v11`; registered 9 Oct before submit; Ido GO 8 Oct ~21:10 "plan_bandit", "in_new_agent")

- **Why.** The plan-as-action agent compares K plans for the same (net, κ) against their shared mean. Its reward must rank plans *within* an instance the way the final fine-tune does. BatchNorm recalibration (EagleEye) is that reward if this cell validates it. v10's own reward (the 12/4 per-step fine-tune) and ft40's (40/10) are measured as proxies on the same plans, which re-tests ft40 (§112) without an agent.
- **Code.** `tree_v11` = `tree_v10l` + two default-off flags. CPU pytest is green on the staged tree (11 new tests, 71 existing). Smoke **22410259** is never quoted.
  - `SPECTRA_ALLOC_KIND=sample`: the alloc walk's plan weights times exp(σ ε_g) per group, around the sens or the uniform weights (`SPECTRA_ALLOC_SAMPLE_AROUND`, `_SIGMA` 0.5, `_SEED`; a private RNG). The plan is bisected to the same params target and cut by the same walk. Each plan's `[alloc]` line and `alloc_plan` record carry the sample.
  - `SPECTRA_EVAL_PROXIES=cut,bn8,bn32,ft1,ft12_4,ft40_10`, measured on a copy of each non-origin TRAJ candidate before its final fine-tune, on the val half, under `SPECTRA_EVAL_PROXY_SEED=0`, with the global RNG restored afterwards:
    - cut is the candidate as cut;
    - bn<N> recalibrates BatchNorm on N train batches;
    - ft<E>_<P> is the per-step fine-tune at E epochs and patience P, with the handler's own recipe (v10's: Adam 1e-3, plateau, select on train loss, GPU crop + flip at batch 256).
    Printed `[proxy]`, recorded `eval_plan_proxy`.
- **Cell.** One-shot alloc walks (`SPECTRA_NUM_EPOCHS=0`, landed params 0.6, 5-rate menu, group-once, 6 passes) under P + G, profile `baseline_c10_alloc_traj_gonce`, candidates saved.
  - *Families and instances.* Thin r56-w4 (`input_c10_thin.json`, with the r20-w2 guard in the same jobs, reported), DepGraph R56 (`input_catalog_l_depgraph_r56.json`) and MobileNetV2 ×0.5 (`input_pf_mbv2x05.json`), κ 0.6. Two instances per family: seed 42 and seed 43 (the val / test split is fixed by `SPECTRA_SPLIT_SEED`).
  - *Plans per instance, one job each.* The named plans sens, uniform and inner, and eight sampled plans at σ 0.5: four around sens and four around uniform, sample seeds 1–8 (instance 42) and 9–16 (instance 43). 66 jobs.
  - *Finals (ground truth).* G at 75 epochs (§334), keep-last, batch 128, under the family's §330 step size (lr 0.1 on R56; lr 0.01 on thin and MobileNetV2), on every candidate. The origin control runs only in each instance's sens job.
  - *Ceiling.* Instance 42's candidate sets are re-finalized with seed 44 (`SPECTRA_EVAL_FINAL_FT_FROM`, afterok, no proxies): 33 jobs.
- **Metric.**
  - Per instance, Spearman ρ between a proxy's val Δ and the final's TEST 5k Δ over the instance's plans, averaged over the two instances.
  - Regret: the instance's best final minus the final of the proxy's top plan, in pp; median over instances.
  - The TEST half is the ground truth so that proxy and truth never share images. This is a diagnostic of rank agreement; no row is chosen by it. The val-half ground truth is reported.
  - The ceiling is ρ between the seed-42 and seed-44 finals over instance 42's plans.
- **Calls, per family.**
  - **CEILING-BOUND** if the ceiling ρ is < 0.60: the final cannot rank that family's plans, so the family neither validates nor vetoes a proxy (reported).
  - Otherwise, per proxy: **VALID** if mean ρ ≥ 0.60 and median regret ≤ 0.5 pp; else **INVALID**.
  - **Reward**: the cheapest proxy (mean minutes) VALID on every family that is not CEILING-BOUND. BatchNorm recalibration is the reward if it is that proxy.
  - If no proxy qualifies, or every family is CEILING-BOUND: report to Ido. The agent would then train on G's full final, and nothing is registered before he reads it.
  - *Reported:*
    - proxy minutes;
    - the finals' spread within each instance;
    - headroom, the best sampled plan minus sens (if no sampled plan beats sens by ≥ 0.3 pp anywhere, there is little to find beyond the sens prior at κ 0.6);
    - sampled-around-sens minus sampled-around-uniform means;
    - sens − uniform and inner − uniform on the finals (D-LEVER's inputs);
    - ft12_4's and ft40_10's validity, as the old rewards' fidelity.
- **Start check.**
  - The `[alloc]` line reads `sample around … sigma=0.5 seed=…` on sampled plans and the named kind otherwise.
  - Six `[proxy]` results per net.
  - The proxies' `Fine-tune recipe` lines read `optim=adam lr=0.001 … schedule=plateau … patience=4 epochs=12` (ft12_4) and `patience=10 epochs=40` (ft40_10).
  - The final's line reads lr 0.01 or 0.1 per family and `select=last`; no NaN.
- **Never.** An agent row or a paper row from this cell; a pick on test; a seed beyond 42 / 43 / 44; a train, resume or freeze TEST pointed at `tree_v11`.
- *Submitted* 01:30–01:33 (`_tmp_s9oct_dproxy_submit.sh`; registered 01:27:00, commit `3abb9c6`), all `Requeue=0`, names `s9-dproxy-<family>-<plan>-k060-s<seed>` and `s9-dproxy-ceil-…-s44`:
  - walks **22411296–22411443** (66; nice 32; afterok on smoke **22410259**), in the order seed 42 thin, R56, MobileNetV2, then seed 43, each as sens, uniform, inner, four sampled around sens, four around uniform;
  - ceiling **22411444–22411479** (33; nice 33; afterok on each seed-42 walk).
  If the smoke's start check fails, the walks are cancelled before they start.

### D-IMIT: can the agent's state and encoder imitate the sens plan, and which representation carries it? (`docs/LEARNING_PROGRAM_OCT8.md` §4; `tree_v12`; registered 9 Oct before submit; Ido GO 8 Oct ~21:10 "plan_bandit" and the encoder rule: reopen every A/B that ran over a uniform policy)

- **Why.** v10 acts like mild (§248) although its reward sees the lever (§259). Without RL, this cell asks whether v10's state and encoder can represent where cutting is safe. If a supervised probe cannot imitate the sens plan from the state, no reward or optimiser will make an agent allocate. It also reopens, without an agent, the representation A/Bs that ran over a uniform or sampled policy: the encoders (§15–§16, C7), group tokens (V8), `STATE_SENS` and group cost (bundled in v10), and skinny nets in training (§17–§18).
- **Code.** `tree_v12` = `tree_v11` + one default-off flag and one script. CPU pytest is green on the staged tree (24 new tests; 42 existing in three suites).
  - `SPECTRA_DUMP_STATES=1`: an eval job does not walk. Per TEST net and per κ in `SPECTRA_DUMP_KEEPS` (0.4, 0.6, 0.8), it resets the environment at that target and saves:
    - the reset state, i.e. the layer tokens the actor reads (63 columns);
    - the same state pooled into group tokens (V8's `group_token_state`);
    - the frozen-BERT input of the same origin (the embeddings path of `SPECTRA_STATE_ENCODER=bert`);
    - the token layout, and each token's planned group (`group_sensitivity`'s mapping);
    - the sens plan at κ − 0.02 (`alloc_walk.plan_targets`, the alloc walk's own call).
    Printed `[dump]`.
  - `scripts/dimit_probe.py`: per arm and fold, the encoder with a per-token linear head (sigmoid) is trained on 8 nets and scored on the 2 held out. A group's prediction is the mean over its tokens; the loss is the group-level MSE to the plan's keeps.
- **Cell.**
  - *Dump* (1 GPU): profile `eval_c10_thin_traj_gonce` with v10's ep0127 actor and critic, read-only. Its `policy_config.json` pins v10's state flags, and the snapshot's standardizer z-scores the base tokens as in v10's train. The 10 nets of `database_offline_v6_p5b2.json` (C10 and the SVHN VGG11), P, `SPECTRA_FT_AUG=1`, seed 42. Not a freeze TEST: the hook returns before any walk, and nothing is quoted.
  - *Probe* (1 GPU, afterok on the dump):
    - recipe: AdamW lr 3e-4, weight decay 0.01, 150 epochs over the training states, one state per step, gradient clip 1, dropout 0; frozen arms are linear probes at lr 1e-3 for 300 epochs;
    - probe seeds 0, 1, 2, averaged per (net, κ); no early stopping; nothing is selected on held-out nets.
  - *Folds* (each holds out 2 of the 10 nets): (r20-w8, VGG11), (r20-w10, MobileNetV2 ×1), (r56-w6, DenseNet40), (R32, SVHN VGG11), (VGG13, MobileNetV2 ×0.5).
  - *Arms:*
    - (a) the default transformer with the sens channels (a sanity check: the rule's input is in the state);
    - (b) the default transformer without the sens channels, the base for the comparisons;
    - (c) the `set` encoder, without sens;
    - (d) the legacy NEON encoder: **not run**. It pools a whole-net feature-map stack into one vector for the current layer, so it has no per-token output and needs a different state;
    - (e) v10's ep0127 actor encoder, frozen, with a linear probe, on the state as v10 reads it. (e0) is its control: the same architecture at random init (seeds 0–2), frozen, linear probe. The effective rank of both encoders' state vectors and token outputs over the dump is reported;
    - (f) `transformer_wide` (6 × 512), without sens;
    - (g) frozen `bert-base-uncased` (cached; `transformers` 4.46.3 is on the cluster; nothing installed): per-layer outputs of the embeddings-mode input, κ appended, linear probe. Reported as not run if the weights do not load; a net past 510 layers is skipped;
    - (h) group tokens, one per coupling group, with the default transformer, without sens;
    - (i) arm (b) with the four group-cost channels zeroed as well;
    - (j) arm (b)'s recipe with the thin nets (r20-w8, r20-w10, r56-w6) out of training, scored on each fold's non-thin held-out nets, against arm (b) on the same nets. Also reported: the thin nets held out with no thin net in training, against arm (b)'s thin held-out reads.
- **Metric.**
  - Per held-out (net, κ): the Spearman between predicted and planned keeps over the planned groups, and the param-weighted mean |Δkeep| (weights: each group's param share from the group-cost channel).
  - Fold mean over its held-out (net, κ); arm mean over the 5 folds.
  - References without training (reported): the state's sens percentile channel, which must read ≈ 1.0 or the target pipeline is broken; token depth; origin width.
- **Calls.**
  - Per arm: **SUFFICIENT** if the mean held-out Spearman is ≥ 0.70, **INSUFFICIENT** if ≤ 0.40, **PARTIAL** between.
  - Fold-paired against a base: **BEATS** if the mean is higher by ≥ 0.10 and it wins on ≥ 4 of 5 folds; **LOSES TO** symmetrically; otherwise **TIES**. Pairs:
    - (a) against (b): the sens channel;
    - (c), (f), (g) and (h) against (b): encoder and token type;
    - (i) against (b): the group cost;
    - (e) against (e0): RL-trained against random features;
    - (j): thin nets out of training against in.
  - *Reading:*
    - (a) not SUFFICIENT: the pipeline is broken, and no other call stands.
    - (b) SUFFICIENT: structure and weight statistics carry allocation beyond the measured channel.
    - (b) INSUFFICIENT with (a) SUFFICIENT: the agent needs the measured sens channel, i.e. a sensitivity pass on every new net, whose cost is part of transfer.
    - (e) SUFFICIENT and BEATS (e0) while v10 acts like mild: the features are there, and the head and optimiser failed. (e) TIES (e0): RL training left nothing beyond random features.
  - *What it decides:* T0's encoder and token type, and whether `STATE_SENS` stays in its state. T2's kickstarting needs (a) SUFFICIENT.
- **Start check (dump).** The `[policy_config]` line pins v10's env; the standardizer is loaded from the snapshot, not skipped; every `[dump]` line reads width 63, `bert=yes` and tokens on planned groups; 30 records. If it fails, the probe is cancelled before it starts.
- **Never.** A TEST or paper row from this cell; a train, resume or freeze TEST pointed at `tree_v12`; a write into v10's run directory.
- *Submitted* 02:07 (`_tmp_s9oct_dimit_submit.sh`; registered 02:05:17, commit `addc932`), both `Requeue=0`: dump **22411734** (`s9-dimit-dump`, nice 20, behind the D-PROXY smoke) and probe **22411735** (`s9-dimit-probe`, afterok on the dump, nice 21; reads `runs/job22411734/state_dump`, writes `runs/job22411734/dimit/dimit_results.json`).

### Plan-agent smoke: does the plan-as-action agent train and plan on the GPU? (`docs/LEARNING_PROGRAM_OCT8.md` §5; `tree_v13`; registered 9 Oct before submit; Ido GO 8 Oct ~21:10 "plan_bandit"; never quoted)

- **Why.** The plan agent of §5 is built. Its first train (T0) is registered only after D-IMIT and D-PROXY report. This smoke checks what the CPU tests cannot: the 10 catalog instances built under v10's pins, the masked reward against a real cut on each real net, the seconds per instance (T0's budget), and the eval path through the alloc walk.
- **Code.** `tree_v13` = `tree_v12` + two default-off flags. CPU pytest is green in the tree (20 new tests; 47 existing in three suites). On five zoo architectures (thin ResNet-20, ResNet-20, MobileNetV2 ×0.5, VGG11-bn, DenseNet40) the analytic param count equals the real cut's, every group lands on its planned width, and the masked cut's outputs equal the real cut's.
  - `SPECTRA_PLAN_TRAIN=1` (`src/plan_trainer.py`): the eval job trains instead of walking. An instance is (net, κ ~ U[0.35, 0.85]).
    - The policy is the default transformer with a per-token head initialised at zero, so it starts at the uniform plan. A group's score is the mean over its tokens.
    - K plans z = μ + σε are decoded: keep = clip(σ(z + b), 0.1, 1), with b bisected so the analytic params hit κ, then a one-channel polish.
    - Each plan is one L1 cut written as zeros on a working copy, read on the val half after `SPECTRA_PLAN_PROXY` (bn32 = D-PROXY's bn<N>, the same seeded batches for all K plans of an instance).
    - REINFORCE with the K-plan mean as baseline. Every `REF_EVERY` instances the mean plan and the uniform / sens / inner plans are scored on the same batches. Policies are saved to `<run dir>/plan_agent/policy_*.pt`.
    - At start, one real cut per net checks the masked reward (`[plan] check`).
  - `SPECTRA_ALLOC_KIND=agent` + `SPECTRA_PLAN_AGENT=<policy>`: the alloc walk follows the frozen agent's mean plan for the net's reset state (`src/plan_agent.py`).
- **Cell.**
  - *Train* (1 GPU): profile `eval_c10_thin_traj_gonce` with v10's ep0127 actor and critic, read-only (its pins and standardizer, as in D-IMIT's dump); the 10 nets of `database_offline_v6_p5b2.json`; P; seed 42. 30 instances at most, K 8, σ 0.5 → 0.2, bn32, mean plan and references every 5 instances, a policy saved every 10, `SPECTRA_PLAN_MAX_MINUTES=18`, the closing summary at κ 0.6; wall 45 min.
  - *Eval* (1 GPU, afterok on the train): the same profile and pins with `SPECTRA_EVAL_POLICY=alloc SPECTRA_ALLOC_KIND=agent` and the smoke's `policy_latest.pt`, on the thin pair (r56-w4, r20-w2) at params 0.6: a one-shot walk and a 2-epoch final fine-tune; wall 40 min.
- **Submitted 9 Oct 02:53** (after registration commit 8426630, 02:51:13): train **22411793** (R 02:53:32, cs-4090-02), eval **22411794** (afterok:22411793); both nice 20, Requeue=0, `rtx_4090`. Submit script `scripts/_tmp_s9oct_plan_smoke.sh` (not in git); provenance in `tree_v13/PROVENANCE_v13_log.txt`. The eval has no G flags (a 2-epoch fine-tune).
- **Start check.**
  - Train: ten `[plan] net` and `[plan] check` lines. On every net the analytic kept equals the real kept and the masked Δ equals the real Δ (no WARNING); dead channels are reported. `[plan] it=` lines carry finite rewards and the seconds per instance; checkpoints are saved; summary lines are printed.
  - Eval: the `[policy_config]` line; the `[alloc] … agent plan_agent/policy_latest.pt` line with no standardizer WARNING; a finished walk and final fine-tune on both nets.
  - If the train's check fails, the eval is cancelled before it starts.
- **Never.** A TEST or paper row from either job; a T0 call read off 30 instances; a train, resume or freeze TEST of the A2C agent pointed at `tree_v13`.

### Morning 9 Oct cells: the single recipe's rows, its VGG check, T0, and the D-IMIT re-run (registered ~08:55 before submit; Ido away ~09:30 for a few hours, so every follow-on is chained by afterok)

Ido gave T0 its GO at 08:45 before D-IMIT reports, because T0 is the single-net control of §5 and D-IMIT's call governs T1's representation.

G2 = `SPECTRA_EVAL_FINAL_FT_EPOCHS=100 SPECTRA_EVAL_FINAL_FT_BATCH=256 SPECTRA_EVAL_FINAL_FT_SELECT=last SPECTRA_EVAL_FINAL_FT_LR=0.1 SPECTRA_FT_AUG_GPU=1 SPECTRA_EVAL_FINAL_FT_CUDA_GRAPH=1` + origin control. Candidates (N02 / N04's walks): thin 22341865 / 67 (inner s42 / 43), 22156062 / 22341278 (mild s42 / 43), 22375992 / 22394258 / 60 (sens s44–46), 22375993 / 22394259 / 61 (uniform), 22395416 / 17 / 21 (inner), 22395413 / 14 / 15 (mild); MobileNetV2 22376486 / 91 (inner s42 / 43), 22376487 / 92 (mild), N04 22409855–866 (s44–46 × sens / uniform / inner / mild). The submit script checks each walk's job name for its arm and seed.

**(D-PROXY-2) registered 19:31:14 before submit (commit `0652644`; code `30ffccc`, `tree_v14`).**
- **Why.** T0 learned from the raw-cut reward (§341). Its plans beat sens in-sample by 22–30 pp on the cut at κ 0.8, yet after the final T0 − sens is +0.11 at κ 0.6. D-PROXY (§340) validated the cut on plans around sens and uniform, not on the plans the trained agent samples, where REINFORCE takes its gradient. If the cut misranks those plans (Goodhart), T1's reward must change. If it ranks them, or the finals cannot tell them apart, the tie with sens is headroom-limited.
- **Design, deviations from the handoff's draft (§9.4 item 4).** Two κ, not one: at κ 0.6 nearly every thin plan sits at the cut's floor (≈ −79 pp, §341 reading 4), while at κ 0.8 the cut has signal and the in-sample lead was largest. A seed-44 ceiling, as in D-PROXY, so that a low ρ can be told apart from noise in the finals.
- **Instances.** T0's cut policies `tree_v13/runs/job22416577` (s42) and `job22416579` (s43) (`plan_agent/policy_latest.pt`, read-only), each at κ 0.6 and 0.8, on the thin pair (`input_c10_thin.json` + `database_c10_thin.json`; r56-w4 votes, r20-w2 reported). Walk and final seed = the instance's seed.
- **Plans per instance (12).**
  - *mean:* `SPECTRA_ALLOC_KIND=agent`, with the origin control;
  - *8 draws:* `SPECTRA_ALLOC_KIND=agent_sample SPECTRA_ALLOC_SAMPLE_SIGMA=0.2` (the train's final σ), sample seeds 1–8 (s42) and 9–16 (s43), the same at both κ;
  - *named:* `SPECTRA_ALLOC_KIND=sens / uniform / inner`.
  - All 12 run T1-E's thin line on `tree_v14`: profile `eval_c10_thin_traj_gonce`, v10 ep0127's pins and standardizer read-only, `SPECTRA_EVAL_POLICY=alloc`, P, one-shot (`SPECTRA_FIXED_TARGET=1 SPECTRA_EVAL_PASSES=6 SPECTRA_EVAL_MIN_PARAM_RATIO=0 SPECTRA_NUM_EPOCHS=0`), `SPECTRA_EVAL_SIZE_MATCH` = `SPECTRA_EVAL_SIZE_POINTS` = `param:<κ>`, G2 final (100 epochs), `SPECTRA_EVAL_SAVE_TRAJ_MODELS=1`, `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1`. The walk mechanics are identical across the 12 plans; only the plan differs.
- **Proxies.** `SPECTRA_EVAL_PROXIES=cut,bn8,bn32,ft1 SPECTRA_EVAL_PROXY_SEED=0`, read on the val half in the cutting job (`src/plan_proxies.py`: copies, RNG saved and restored, so the final is not moved).
- **Ceiling.** Instance s42's 12 candidate sets at each κ re-finalized under G2 with fine-tune seed 44 (`SPECTRA_EVAL_FINAL_FT_FROM`, profile `baseline_c10_mild_traj_gonce`, `tree_v14`), afterok on each walk.
- **Jobs.** 48 walks + 24 ceilings = 72, plus the smoke. nice 26 (ahead of VG2's 30, behind T1-E and K8), wall 2 h (walks) / 1.5 h (ceilings), `rtx_4090`, Requeue=0. Names `s9-dp2-k<060|080>-<plan>-s<42|43>` and `s9-dp2-ceil-k<060|080>-<plan>-s44`. About 9–10 GPU-h.
- **Smoke (never quoted).** One `agent_sample` walk (s42 policy, sample seed 1, κ 0.6) with a 2-epoch final, nice 19, wall 45 min. Every cell waits on it by afterok. Start check: the `[alloc]` line reads `agent_sample sigma=0.2 seed=1 around the mean of job22416577/policy_latest.pt` with plan keeps ≈ x0.580; `[proxy]` lines for cut / bn8 / bn32 / ft1 on both nets; a `TRAJ final_ft` line; no Traceback or NaN. If it fails, every dependent is cancelled before it starts.
- **Read.** Spearman within each instance over its 9 agent plans: proxy (val Δ) against the G2 final (TEST 5k Δ). Regret = best final among the 9 − the final of the proxy's top pick. Ceiling = Spearman between the seed-42 and seed-44 finals over instance s42's 9 agent plans. Calls in the row.
- **Never.** A D-PROXY-2 number as a TEST row of the agent (the finals rank plans; they are not an agent result); a proxy adopted as the reward on this read without Ido; adding draws or seeds after the read.

**(T0-F) registered ~20:40 before submit (code `b86e505`, `tree_v15`; Ido's GO for the train, confirmed 18:56).**
- **Why.** At equal params, T0 ties sens but keeps 1.24–1.37× its FLOPs, because a params budget rewards cutting the late, wide stage (§341). Structured-pruning papers set their targets in FLOPs or latency (handoff §6.4). The literature scan (`docs/LIT_SCAN_9OCT_TRANSFER_BUDGET.md`) finds no pruning policy that takes the budget type as an input; T0-F is the single-type control that a mixed-budget agent would need. On thin r56-w4 the three stages cost about the same MACs per conv (32²·4², 16²·8², 8²·16² per position), while params concentrate in stage 3. So the FLOPs lever need not resemble the params lever; that is the question.
- **Code** (`tree_v15`, every flag off by default):
  - `plan_agent.FlopModel`: Conv/Linear MACs from the group cuts times each module's spatial size; BatchNorm/activation MACs linear per group, one probe cut each. Exact against `utils.calc_flops` of the real cut on all five zoo nets (`tests/test_v15_flops_budget.py`, 26 green on the login node).
  - `SPECTRA_PLAN_BUDGET=flops`: κ is a fraction of MACs, and the state flags the budget in the first action slot.
  - `SPECTRA_ALLOC_BUDGET=flops`: plans and reference bisection in MACs.
  - `SPECTRA_FIXED_TARGET_METRIC=flop`: the eval's episode target, landing and done check in MACs. It is not a policy contract key, so v10's pins cannot override it.
- **(T0-F train) 3 jobs**, seeds 42 / 43 / 44. T0's train line exactly (§341: thin pair input, `SPECTRA_PLAN_NETS=resnet56-width4`, 3,000 instances, K 8, σ 0.5 → 0.2 over 60 %, lr 3e-4, batch 4, κ ~ U[0.35, 0.85], `k_min` 0.1, proxy cut, `NORM_ADV=0`, references every 50, saves every 250, summary at κ 0.4 / 0.6 / 0.8, `MAX_MINUTES=300`, v10 ep0127's pins and standardizer read-only, profile `eval_c10_thin_traj_gonce`, wall 6 h, nice 24), plus `SPECTRA_PLAN_BUDGET=flops`.
- **(T0-F eval) 3 jobs**, each afterok on its train. T0's eval line, with `SPECTRA_ALLOC_BUDGET=flops SPECTRA_FIXED_TARGET_METRIC=flop SPECTRA_EVAL_SIZE_MATCH=flop:0.6 SPECTRA_EVAL_SIZE_POINTS=flop:0.6`: the thin pair, one-shot (`SPECTRA_FIXED_TARGET=1 SPECTRA_EVAL_PASSES=6 SPECTRA_EVAL_MIN_PARAM_RATIO=0 SPECTRA_NUM_EPOCHS=0`), G2 + origin, seed = the train's seed, nice 24, wall 2 h.
- **(sens_F / uniform_F / inner_F) 9 jobs**, seeds 42–44: the eval line above with `SPECTRA_ALLOC_KIND=sens|uniform|inner`, so the walk mechanics equal the agent's. nice 25, wall 2 h.
- **(mild_F) 3 walks + 3 G2 re-finals**, seeds 42–44. K8's mild-landed line (`baseline_c10_mild_traj_gonce`, `SPECTRA_FIXED_TARGET=1 SPECTRA_EVAL_PASSES=6 SPECTRA_EVAL_MIN_PARAM_RATIO=0`, the walk's own per-step fine-tune and final, origin) with `SPECTRA_FIXED_TARGET_METRIC=flop` and `flop:0.6`, on `tree_v15`. Each walk is followed by afterok by a G2 re-final of its saved candidates, as K8's comparators were. nice 25; wall 1 d (walk), 3 h (re-final).
- **Smokes (never quoted), nice 19.**
  - (S1) a train of 24 instances (`SPECTRA_PLAN_BUDGET=flops`, references every 6, summary at κ 0.6).
  - (S2) S1's policy through the eval line with a 2-epoch final, afterok on S1.
  - (S3) a sens_F one-shot with a 2-epoch final.
  - Every T0-F cell waits on S2 and S3 by afterok.
  - Start checks:
    - `[plan] check-flops` on r56-w4 with analytic = real kept MACs, no WARNING;
    - `b=flops` on the `[plan] it=` lines, finite rewards, `[plan] DONE`;
    - in S2 / S3, "fixed target: keep x0.600 of the FLOPs", `[alloc] … of the FLOPs`, a "lands at FLOPs x…" or "episode ends at FLOPs x…" line, `TRAJ final_ft size_flop0.6` rows, and no Traceback or NaN.
  - If any check fails, the dependents are cancelled before they start.
- **About 10 GPU-h.** Trains ~1 h each (T0 took 51–53 min), evals and one-shots ~15 min, mild walks a few hours each.
- **Never.** A train-log value as TEST; an equal-params claim from these rows; T0-F seeds beyond 42–44; adopting a FLOPs budget for T1 on this read (that goes to Ido).

### Night 10 Oct cells (Claude Code; Ido asleep and authorised the Recommended option on every fork until ~13:00; registered 03:57 before submit, commit `99806a0`; submitted 03:59:48–04:03:32)

- **Decisions recorded (Ido, 10 Oct ~03:20).**
  1. "I'll take the +0.28 as if BEATS-PRIOR has fired; let's continue on that avenue." Recorded as a programme decision: the transfer avenue continues. §346's registered call stays as read (not BEATS-PRIOR: +0.28 against +0.30). BEATS-PRIOR is tested again only in registered cells, preferably where the lever after the final is large (§348, §349).
  2. Family-level hold-out: "I want both options together, I want a wider held-out validation over the next experiments." → HF (frozen T1 on families that no catalog holds) and LOFO (retrain without one family).
  3. Mixed-budget agent: GO after T0-F's read. T0-F came back FAILS-CONTROL (§350), so the mixed train is held until a FLOPs-capable reward exists; D-PROXY-F is registered next (Recommended, under Ido's authorisation).
  4. Cost-aware sensitivity baseline: GO → `sens_cost`, new code in `tree_v16`, registered next.
  5. CIFAR-100 in the training pool → T1-C.
  6. Brief at ~10:00; no blocking questions before ~13:00.
- **Grounding.** `docs/CORNERSTONE_DIGEST_10OCT.md` (the three cornerstone PDFs, read in full): NEON held out datasets in a 5-fold rotation and never an architecture family; the proposal (p.15) asks for unseen architectures on unfamiliar datasets. HF-N tests unseen families on a seen dataset, HF-FM unseen families on an unseen dataset, HF-V an unseen dataset and depth within a seen family, and LOFO the rotation over families.
- **Common line.** `tree_v13` (T1's tree; the seven new configs below are its only change, logged in `PROVENANCE_v13_log.txt`); profile `eval_c10_thin_traj_gonce`; v10 ep0127's actor / critic pins and standardizer read-only; P (`SPECTRA_VAL_FROM_TEST=1 SPECTRA_BATCH_SIZE=256 SPECTRA_FT_AUG=1 SPECTRA_EVAL_DETERMINISTIC=1 SPECTRA_EVAL_FINAL_FT_ORIGIN=1 SPECTRA_EVAL_SAVE_TRAJ_MODELS=1`); one-shot (`SPECTRA_FIXED_TARGET=1 SPECTRA_EVAL_PASSES=6 SPECTRA_EVAL_MIN_PARAM_RATIO=0 SPECTRA_NUM_EPOCHS=0`); G2 final (§348); `rtx_4090`; Requeue=0; seeds 42–44, with `SPECTRA_SEED` = the policy's seed.
  - Agent evals: `SPECTRA_EVAL_POLICY=alloc SPECTRA_ALLOC_KIND=agent SPECTRA_PLAN_AGENT=<run>/plan_agent/policy_latest.pt`. Comparators: the same line with `SPECTRA_ALLOC_KIND=sens|uniform`, so the walk mechanics equal the agent's. Never an agent under a baseline profile.
  - Per net, INPUT = DATABASE = its one-net config, with `SPECTRA_DATASET_NAMES` = cifar-10 / cifar-100 / fashion-mnist; DepGraph R56 at params 0.47 and the thin pair at 0.6 exactly as T1-E.
  - Trains (LOFO-R, LOFO-V, T1-C): T1's line (row 99: 12,000 instances, K 8, σ 0.5 → 0.2 over 60 %, lr 3e-4, batch 4, κ ~ U[0.35, 0.85], `k_min` 0.1, proxy cut, `NORM_ADV=0`, references every 50, saves every 500, summary at κ 0.4 / 0.6 / 0.8, `MAX_MINUTES=480`, wall 9 h) with exactly one change: `SPECTRA_PLAN_NETS` (LOFO) or the catalog and dataset names (T1-C). With `NORM_ADV=0` and no per-net normalisation, C100 instances may weigh more in T1-C's gradient [H]; kept as is to keep one change.
- **Configs** (committed with this registration; generated from existing files): `input_novel_c10_{sn1,sn15,rva0,rva1}.json` (from `input_offline_novel.json`), `input_holdout_fm_{sn1,rva0}.json` (from `input_g2_holdout_fmnist.json`), `database_v6_plus_c100.json` (`database_offline_v6_p5b2.json` plus the 8 C100 nets of `database_offline_v7_diverse_admitted.json`).

Every cell waits on the smokes of its paths by afterok; if a smoke fails, science cancels its dependents.

About 90 GPU-h in all. *09:03: Slurm's age factor (counted from eligibility) outweighed these small nice gaps, so HF-FM (eligible since 04:08) ran ahead of the LOFO-R evals, sens_cost and T0-F2 (eligible ~07:30). The 8 HF-FM jobs still pending were re-niced to 200 to restore the registered order; nothing was cancelled.*

**(sens_cost) registered 05:08:32 before submit (commit `e383200`, submitted 05:09:12; `tree_v16` = `tree_v15` + code `d240176`; PROVENANCE_v16 lists the three files and their blobs).**

At equal params T0 and T1 keep 1.2–1.6× sens's FLOPs (§341, §346); under a FLOPs budget sens_F is the strongest rule on r56-w4 (§350). A rule that prices each cut by what it saves is the comparator any FLOPs or mixed-budget agent must beat.

Seeds 42–44; profile `eval_c10_thin_traj_gonce`, v10 ep0127 pins, P, one-shot, G2 + origin, as the agent's evals.

**(T0-F2, T0-F-W) registered 05:59:42 before submit (commit `4dd6395`, submitted 06:00:38; `tree_v17` = `tree_v16` + code `136aa59`; PROVENANCE_v17 lists the files and blobs).**
- **Why.** The v17 probe (CPU, login node, from the saved state dicts and records) found T0-F's failure mechanism [V]: the plan decoders allowed width 1, the eval walk never cuts a group below 2 (`fortify.min_width_for_prune`); the FLOPs agent learned width-1 stage-1 groups on r56-w4 (one channel there buys 4× the MACs of a stage-3 channel); the walk floored them at 2, ended 5 % of the FLOPs above budget, and its stall fallback cut the stage-2 residual stream (8 → 6) to the chance floor. The −57.8 → −78.4 in-sample-to-eval drop splits into −6.8 (the κ 0.6 → 0.58 undershoot), −2.2 (the floor) and −11.1 (the fallback). Re-decoding the trained s42 policy with the floor ties sens_F on the cut. T0 under params plans no width-1 group, so it was untouched.
- **Code.** `SPECTRA_PLAN_MIN_WIDTH=<int>|walk` floors every plan decoder: the trainer's sampled plans, mean plan and references; `plan_for_env` (the flag, else the floor written into the policy blob); `alloc_walk.plan_targets` (the rule comparators). Unset = `tree_v16` (tests: `test_v17_plan_min_width.py` 11, `test_plan_agent.py` + `test_v16_sens_cost.py` 32, `test_v15_flops_budget.py` 26, on `stage_v17`).

Floored comparators (9): sens_F / uniform_F / inner_F on the same line. `rtx_4090`, Requeue=0.
- **Smokes (never quoted), nice 19.** (S1) a 24-instance train with the flag; (S2) S1's policy through the floored eval line with a 2-epoch final, afterok on S1. Every cell waits on S1 and S2 by afterok. Start checks: the `[plan] trainer` line ends with `min_width=2`; a `[plan] check-flops` line with analytic = real; `[plan] DONE`; in S2 the `[alloc]` line ends with `; group width floor 2`, a landing line, no "strongest legal cut from here", `TRAJ final_ft size_flop0.60` and origin rows, no Traceback or NaN.

**(TM) registered 13:10:44 before submit (commit `9f46846`, submitted 13:11:47; `tree_v17`; Ido's GO for the mixed-budget agent, 10 Oct ~03:20; prerequisite met by §357).**

T0-F2 shows the plan agent can learn under a FLOPs budget once plans are realizable (§357); TM asks whether one catalog-trained agent keeps T1's params skill while also planning FLOPs.

Start checks: the `[plan] trainer` line shows `"budget": "mixed"` and ends with `min_width=2`; `[plan] check` and `check-flops` lines with analytic = real; `it=` lines with and without ` b=flops`; `[plan] DONE`; in S2 the `[alloc]` line ends with `; group width floor 2`, a landing line, no stall fallback, `TRAJ final_ft size_flop0.60` and origin rows, no Traceback, ERROR or NaN.

### Evening 10 Oct cells (Claude Code; registered 17:20; `tree_v18`)

**(T2-F) registered 17:20 before any submit; trains and evals need Ido's GO.**
- **Why.** §360: TM FAILS, and its plans barely use the budget flag. The FLOPS_FIRST FAILS row names a FLOPs-primary train with a residual on sens_F as the next cell. T2 starts at the rule (its zero-init scores reproduce sens_F's plan) and can only learn a change to it. So the read asks the question the thesis needs: does a frozen learned policy add anything to the best heuristic on a net it never saw?
- **Line.**
  - Train: TM's `TRAIN` line with `SPECTRA_PLAN_BUDGET=flops` and `SPECTRA_PLAN_RESIDUAL=sens` (`SPECTRA_PLAN_RESIDUAL_ALPHA` / `_SIGMA` at their defaults, 0.5 / 0.25). Catalog `database_offline_v6_p5b2.json` (C10 + SVHN), 12,000 instances, summary κ 0.4 / 0.6 / 0.8.

- **Never.** Trains or evals before Ido's GO. A wide-net eval before grid rounding is deployed. "The agent beats sens_F" unless BEATS-PRIOR-F fires. Seeds beyond 42–44. A train-log value as TEST. Quoting a smoke.

**(T2-F-W) registered 19:10 before submit; the follow-on block of T2-F (Ido's GO ~18:15).**
- **Why.** The registered T2-F call reads r56-w4, the one net where floored arms land without grid rounding. The wide nets need grid rounding: their walks stall on the cut-rate grid (§352 note). This block gives the FLOPs-matched DepGraph R56 star (GO-Q) and settles VGG-19 C100's FLOPs headroom (protocol §8 item 6).
- **Line.** `tree_v19`; every arm `SPECTRA_PLAN_MIN_WIDTH=walk SPECTRA_ALLOC_GRID_ROUND=1`, G2, one-shot. Points: DepGraph R56 `flop:0.48`, VGG-19 C100 `flop:0.6`. Agents: `tree_v18/runs/job<T2-F train>/plan_agent/policy_latest.pt`, afterok on 22484944 / 951 / 954.

**(T3-F) registered 21:39 before any submit; needs Ido's GO.**
- **Why.** T2-F's learned residual helps VGG-19 C100 (+0.34) and hurts both held-out ResNets (−1.89, −0.53; §361, §362). [H] Under `NORM_ADV=0` (raw advantage, inherited from T0's single-net line), instances whose proxy rewards span tens of pp (VGG / MobileNet / DenseNet) dominate the gradient. `NORM_ADV=1` divides each instance's advantage by its K-plan spread, so every net weighs the same. One change, so the read attributes it.
- **Line.** `tree_v19`.
  - Train: T2-F's line with `SPECTRA_PLAN_NORM_ADV=1`.
  - Evals: r56-w4 as in T2-F (floor, no grid); DepGraph R56 `flop:0.48` and VGG-19 C100 `flop:0.6` as in T2-F-W (floor + `SPECTRA_ALLOC_GRID_ROUND=1`).

The eval must show its `residual on sens` line and land near FLOPs 0.58–0.60 with no fallback. Cells are submitted held and released after the read.
- **Cost.** About 13 GPU-h: 3 trains × ~3.3 h; 9 evals × ~0.3 h.
- **Never.** A submit before Ido's GO. Mixing grid-rounded and unrounded rows in one comparison. "The agent beats sens_F" unless call (1) fires. Seeds beyond 42–44.

Grid rounding now lands every arm exactly (§362). The recorded calls are re-read as whole comparisons, never one patched arm, because rounding also moves plans that never stalled.

### Transfer coverage protocol (design stub, Claude Code, 10 Oct 16:31; `docs/TRANSFER_COVERAGE_PROTOCOL.md`; nothing registered, nothing submitted)

- **Gate.** No row below is registered until a sufficient agent exists (protocol §5.A: LEARNS on its train budget and ≥ −0.3 vs floored sens_F at FLOPs 0.6 on r56-w4; for a residual agent BEATS-PRIOR-F, ≥ +0.3, Ido 10 Oct ~18:15) and Ido GOs the row. §360 (TM) picks the branch (protocol §7).
- **Common line.** The sufficient agent's recipe with one change per fold; the floor on in every arm and plans rounded onto the walk's cut grid (`SPECTRA_ALLOC_GRID_ROUND`, in development); G2 + origin; one-shot mean plan; seeds 42–44, then 45–46 for every fold in the paper table (both stages registered up front); TEST 5k with val, 10k and honest; FLOPs headline, params beside; zero stall-fallback lines, zero masked edits, landing within 0.01.
- **Stub rows, in order** (each becomes its own registration):
  1. HYG: floored, grid-rounded re-reads of the fallback comparators (R56C uniform s44–46; HF-V uniform; TM's VGG-19 sens_F / uniform_F) and of TM's unfloored params comparators; floored sens_cost_F / inner_F where missing. No train. ~10–12 GPU-h. Waits for `SPECTRA_ALLOC_GRID_ROUND`.
  2. SCR: headroom screens (floored sens_F − uniform_F, ≥ 2 seeds, read on val) for every protocol §3 candidate; ShuffleNetV2 realizability with `SPECTRA_PLAN_STRUCTURAL_ONLY`. ~40–55 GPU-h.
  3. REF: the sufficient agent on the held-out instances that passed SCR.
  4. LOFO-M, LOFO-D: callable families only; the family list (ShuffleNetV2 in or out) fixed before submit.
  5. LOFO-R′, LOFO-V′: one recipe across folds [D], fold-fitted standardizers.
  6. DO-C100, DO-FMNIST: REF on held-out datasets (evals only); κ chosen on val.
  7. DO-SVHN: the reference catalog minus VGG-11 SVHN.
  8. SN: every fold's agent on ShuffleNetV2, if SCR passed.
  9. LADDER: 6 / 10 / 18 nets at 12,000 instances, sufficient recipe only.
- **Calls (proposed, protocol §4).** FAMILY-TRANSFER-F (agent − floored uniform_F ≥ +0.5), AT-PRIOR-F (agent − prior_F ≥ −0.3), BEATS-PRIOR-F (≥ +0.3, no callable net below −0.3); no callable net → LEVER-LIMITED.
- **Never.** Protocol §9.

## O38 reward replay (zero GPU, val only; 1 Oct 03:10)

`scripts/reward_replay.py <run_dir...> --tau 10 --gamma 1` replays finished no-agent walks through the live `src.utils.compute_reward` under each shape. Identity steps earn no size credit. Return = undiscounted sum along the walk; in brackets, the share of the positive return paid on cuts with cumulative val Δ > 0 (the gain arm). Not a TEST.

| Walk | Cuts: gain / band / miss | Ends (keep, val Δ) | Live `cbrt_cubes` | Cubic gain `cbrt_miss` (C1) | NEON raw, realised ρ (C2) | Full `cbrt` | NEON literal, nominal ρ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| N3 DG R56, P + aug 40/10, 5-pass (21767189) | 150: 69 / 81 / 0 | 0.356, −1.10 | 101.4 (33 %) | 493.1 (86 %) | 493.1 (86 %) | 94.7 (36 %) | 6.98e4 (99 %) |
| DG R56, no aug (21730500; 21726340) | 150: 0 / 150 / 0 | 0.356, −4.62; −4.04 | 101.4 | 101.4 | 101.4 | 105.2 | 1500 |
| thin aug 40/10, r20-w2 (21729557) | 16: 6 / 10 / 0 | 0.522, −4.34 | 102.9 (31 %) | 2267 (97 %) | 2267 (97 %) | 51.2 (63 %) | 6100 (98 %) |
| thin aug 40/10, r56-w4 (21729557) | 60: 0 / 60 / 0 | 0.622, −4.34 | 126.6 | 126.6 | 126.6 | 60.5 | 600 |
| thin aug 12/4, r20-w2 (21729556) | 16: 3 / 13 / 0 | 0.522, −5.18 | 102.9 (7 %) | 134.2 (29 %) | 134.2 (29 %) | 31.8 (22 %) | 3130 (96 %) |
| thin aug 12/4, r56-w4 (21729556) | 60: 0 / 60 / 0 | 0.622, −5.30 | 126.6 | 126.6 | 126.6 | 60.5 | 600 |
| aug C100 gate 12/4, the 7 nets without a miss (21729554) | 18–76: 0 gain, 0 miss | 0.658–0.696, −1.18 to −8.84 | 36.1–46.4 | = live | = live | 21.7–58.1 | 180–760 |
| aug C100 gate 12/4, r56-w9 (21729554) | 60: 0 / 52 / 8 | 0.642, −10.46 | 18.04 | 18.04 | **−368.9** | 22.5 | **−7480** |

Every shape's return peaks at the walk's last cut, except on r56-w9: cut 51 of 60 (keep 0.742, val −7.66), NEON literal at 45 (0.756, −9.44).

1. **The live reward is blind to Δacc inside the band.** With no miss it pays Σρ: the aug and no-aug R56 walks both score 101.4 with a 3.5 pp val gap.
2. Full cbrt pays the no-aug walk more than the aug walk (105.2 vs 94.7): ρ^⅓ > ρ for ρ < 1, so it rewards many tiny cuts.
3. **C1 = C2 on every walk without a miss.** The two trains differ only on misses (−ρ vs −ρ³). One 7.3 % cut 0.76 pp past τ costs −7.3 live, −394 raw.
4. Cubic-gain credit sits on a few large cuts made above the origin (86–97 % of the return from ρ > 1 cuts); tiny cuts earn ≈ 0. C1 pushes toward large cuts while the net is above origin.
5. τ = 10 binds on 1 of 15 walks. The shapes differ in what they pay along a walk, not in where a return-maximiser stops.
6. At the train FT (12/4) the gain arm is rare: 3 of 76 thin cuts, none on C100. C1 may see few gain-arm steps.
7. Band miss ties live on r56-w9 by coincidence (18.036 vs 18.044). It also charges identity steps taken past τ; live charges them 0.
